Algorithm pair_subproblem(h,n,p,q,s):
- r0=p, r1=q. affected m range min(n-1-p, n-1-q) to max(2n-2-p, 2n-2-q). For adjacent, length ~n+p+1.
- ms = arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
- For each of three a_values (0,0.5,1) maybe (or Alo,Ahi? but polynomial degree 2 so any 3). Set vp=v, vq=s-v. Need compute total contribution to each affected m from terms where either p or q is one of the two indices (including both). We can compute by summing over i? For each m (k=m-(n-1)), the correlation M_k over domain includes all i. Contributions involving p or q:
   If p as first: i=p, j=p+k; if 0≤j<n: term h_p*(1-h_j) with h_j = h[new] at j (maybe q if j=q).
   If p as second: i=p-k; if 0≤i<n: term h_i*(1-h_p) with h_i = new if i=q.
   Similarly q first/second.
We can vectorize over ms. For each test value, create arrays h_p=vp, h_q=vq. Need h_i values for i = p-k, p+k, q-k, q+k with endpoint restrictions; if i is p/q use vp/vq, else fixed h[i]. Since p,q adjacent, q can be p+1. We can compute fixed arrays for coefficients maybe.
Simpler: Since only p,q, define fixed h_fixed = h with p,q=0. For each ms, compute for each role:
For p_first: j=p+k. valid j in [0,n). h_j = h_fixed[j] + (vp if j==p else vq if j==q else 0). But j==p only k=0 (diag), j==q if k=1 (cross). term = vp*(1 - h_j).
For p_second: i=p-k. valid. h_i similarly. term = h_i*(1 - vp).
Similarly q roles. Then new_contrib = sum terms. old_contrib = same with old vp0, vq0. Delta_test = new_contrib - old_contrib. Then c_test = c[ms] + delta_test (c current full). Since outside unchanged.
- Fit quadratic for each m: given y0=c_test at a0? Wait c_test includes c current plus delta relative to original. We need polynomial f(a). We can sample at three a values v0, v1, v2 (e.g., 0, 0.5, 1) and compute f_values = c_full_forם? But c_test uses delta relative to current original; if sample v=old a? We can just compute f_values directly: For each a_sample, compute c_sample = c_current + delta(a_sample). We don't need extrapolate from old; f(a) is absolute value. Fit coefficients Q such that Q0+Q1 a+Q2 a^2 = f(a) at samples. Since degree ≤2 exactly. Use samples a=[0,0.5,1] (or Alo,Ahi, midpoint). If any sample outside [0,1]? all inside. Fit: Q2 = 2*(f0 - f1 + f2)? Let's solve for a=0, a=1, a=0.5? Let values y0=y(0), y1=y(0.5), y2=y(1). For quadratic y = A + B a + C a^2.
y0=A.
y1=A+0.5B+0.25C.
y2=A+B+C.
B = (y2-y0 -0.5C). Solve: y2-y0 = B+C = B+2(y1-y0-0.5B?) Let's derive: y1-y0=0.5B+0.25C -> 4(y1-y0)=2B+C. y2-y0=B+C. Difference: 4(y1-y0)-(y2-y0)=B. So B=4y1 -4y0 - y2 + y0 = 4y1 -3y0 - y2. C = (y2-y0)-B = y2-y0 -4y1+3y0+y2 = 2y0 -4y1 +2y2 = 2(y0 -2y1 + y2). A=y0. Good.
Use sample a=[0,0.5,1] regardless of feasible interval.
- Then we need find minimal L over a∈[Alo,Ahi] of max_m(Q0_m + Q1_m a + Q2_m a^2). Use bisection L.
Feasibility for given L: Need ∃a in [Alo,Ahi] such that all quadratics ≤L.
For each quadratic: f(a)≤L.
- If Q2≈0: linear interval constraint.
- If Q2>0 (convex): f≤L gives a between roots (or empty if L<min). Need intersect interval. If discriminant <0: either always ≤L if Q2>0 and vertex>L? For Q2>0, f-L = Q2a^2+Q1a+(Q0-L). If disc<0 and Q2>0, always positive -> infeasible (since f>L for all a). If L above min, disc≥0. For Q2>0, feasible interval [r1,r2] within.
- If Q2<0 (concave): f≤L gives a outside roots if disc≥0; if disc<0, f-L always negative? For Q2<0, quadratic opens down; f-L<0 all a if max<L, feasible all. roots if disc≥0, feasible (-∞,r1]∪[r2,∞).
We also have linear interplay. Feasible set in 1D can be union of intervals. Checking feasibility exactly with many quadratics: Starting with interval [Alo,Ahi], linear/convex constraints intersect to a single interval; concave constraints may cut out middle intervals, resulting multiple intervals. If we only intersect convex and linear, then check concave cuts? Convex constraints yield interval; concave feasible is outside intervals, so intersection of an interval with several outside sets can be multiple intervals. - Cross lags (m for shift ±1): include product a(1-b) or b(1-a): Q2=+1 convex.
- Other lags: no pair interaction; Q2=0 linear.
Thus only one concave constraint (diag) and at most two convex cross if p,q within range (always cross two lags if adjacent) and maybe if pairs at boundaries still both? Adjacent always cross at ±1 but one may be outside? For full lags, both positive k=1 and -1 exist if p,q valid; yes. So constraints: one concave diagonal, up to two convex cross, rest linear. Feasibility vectorization details:
M length; diag mask = (ms == n-1). Nondiag arrays.
For linear/convex we can simply process all nondiag with Q2.
Tol small 1e-12.
Pseudo:
def pair_feasible(Q0,Q1,Q2,diag_mask,L,Alo,Ahi):
    lo=Alo; hi=Ahi
    non = ~diag_mask
    if np.any(non):
        q0=Q0[non]; q1=Q1[non]; q2=Q2[non]
        lin = np.abs(q2) < 1e-10
        if np.any(lin):
            a=q1[lin]; b=q0[lin]
            if np.any(b > L + 1e-12): return False
            pos = a > 1e-12
            if np.any(pos): hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - b[pos])/a[pos])))
            neg = a < -1e-12
            if np.any(neg): lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - b[neg])/a[neg])))
        if lo > hi + 1e-12: return False
        conv = q2 > 1e-10
        if np.any(conv):
            qc0=q0[conv]; qc1=q1[conv]; qc2=q2[conv]
            disc = qc1*qc1 - 4*qc2*(qc0 - L)
            if np.any(disc < -1e-10):
                # for tiny negative set 0? If disc<0 infeasible
                # But due rounding maybe set 0 if close
                disc = np.where(disc < 0, 0.0, disc)
            sqrt = np.sqrt(disc)
            r1 = (-qc1 - sqrt)/(2*qc2)
            r2 = (-qc1 + sqrt)/(2*qc2)
            # qc2>0 so r1<=r2
            lo = max(lo, float(np.max(r1)))
            hi = min(hi, float(np.min(r2)))
        if lo > hi + 1e-12: return False
    # diag scalar
    d = np.where(diag_mask)[0][0] if any else None
    if d is not None:
        d0=Q0[d]; d1=Q1[d]; d2=Q2[d]
        # If diag is essentially linear? not.
        if d2 >= -1e-10:
            # treat as linear? if concave not; but diag should negative.
            if d1 > 1e-12: hi=min(hi,(L-d0)/d1)
            elif d1 < -1e-12: lo=max(lo,(L-d0)/d1)
            elif d0 > L+1e-12: return False
        else:
            disc = d1*d1 - 4*d2*(d0 - L)
            if disc < 0:
                # Should be all feasible? For d2<0. But check if not:
                # evaluate at midpoint? If f_mid <=L all? Since no roots and downward -> all <L if discriminant<0.
                pass
            else:
                sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
                r1=(-d1 - sqrt)/(2*d2)
                r2=(-d1 + sqrt)/(2*d2)
                if r1>r2: swap
                # feasible outside [r1,r2]
                if not (lo <= min(hi, r1) + 1e-12 or max(lo, r2) <= hi + 1e-12): return False
    # after diag, also ensure linear/convex? already.
    return True

But note for convex disc <0: if q2>0 and disc<0, quadratic q0+q1 a+q2 a^2 - L is always positive (upward no roots) -> infeasible. So return False if disc < -tol. If disc negative tiny, set 0 giving point root.

For concave diag disc<0, always feasible? Let's test d2<0, f-L = d2 a^2 + d1 a + d0-L. Leading negative. If no real roots, sign is negative for all a (since as ±∞ negative and no crossing). Thus f<L all a. Good.

   c=...
   no_improve=0
   phase=0
   while time.time() < t_end:
      if phase%10? choose mode: even, odd, all, random batch.
      improved=False

def pair_find_a(Q0,Q1,Q2,diag_idx,Alo,Ahi,old_a,low,high,iters=30):
    M=Q0.size
    non = np.ones(M, dtype=bool)
    if diag_idx>=0: non[diag_idx]=False
    q0 = Q0[non]; q1=Q1[non]; q2=Q2[non]
    tol=1e-9
    lin = np.abs(q2) < tol
    pos = lin & (q1 > tol)
    neg = lin & (q1 < -tol)
    zero = lin & (~pos) & (~neg)
    zero_max = float(np.max(q0[zero])) if zero.any() else 0.0
    conv = q2 > tol
    c0 = q0[conv]; c1 = q1[conv]; c2 = q2[conv]
    # If any non-diag concave, process in check? We'll store arrays and ignore if none.
    conc = q2 < -tol
    if conc.any():
       # To be safe, include as outside intervals maybe by brute forcing? We'll handle in check by applying to interval after base: for each concave (few) split.
    d_exists = diag_idx>=0
    d0=...; d1=...; d2=...
    low = max(low, zero_max)
    for it in range(iters):
       mid = 0.5*(low+high)
       ok=True
       if mid < zero_max - 1e-10: ok=False
       if ok and conv.any():
           disc = c1*c1 - 4*c2*(c0 - mid)
           if np.any(disc < -1e-8): ok=False
           else:
              disc = np.maximum(disc,0.0)
              sqrt = np.sqrt(disc)
              r1 = (-c1 - sqrt)/(2*c2)
              r2 = (-c1 + sqrt)/(2*c2)
              lo = max(Alo, float(np.max(r1)))
              hi = min(Ahi, float(np.min(r2)))
              if lo > hi + 1e-10: ok=False
           # But linear constraints also; need combine with linear, not just conv. So maintain lo/hi across all.
```
Need combine constraints in one pass. We'll initialize lo=Alo, hi=Ahi. For conv, update lo/hi. Then linear. Then diag/concave outside. Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=1.0; best_n=0
    # parent
    n0=0
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
       n0=len(h0)
    except: n0=0
    if n0>0:
       h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
       c0=compute_c5(h0,n0); update
       # pair optimize parent with 45% budget maybe
       hopt=pair_optimize(h0,n0, min(0.45*budget_s, 400), seed, random_trials=2000)
       update
       if time < deadline: final small SLSQP? maybe not
    # define candidate n and starts
    n_list = [700 if parent? not duplicate, 350, 250, 200, 150, 100, 64]
    # Use budget remaining. For each n if time left > 60:
       # starts:
       # - interpolate best_h to n
       # - parent if parent exists interpolate
       # - 2 random binary
       # - 2 pattern starts
       # For each start with time left: pair_optimize with time slice maybe 60-150s for small.
       # update best
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Pair_optimize time control:
```
def pair_optimize(h,n,time_budget,seed,random_batch=300, cycles_pattern=None):
   t_end=time.time()+time_budget
   h=h.copy(); c=np.correlate...
   no_improve=0
   cycle=0
   while time.time()<t_end:
      if cycle%5==0: mode even
      elif cycle%5==1: odds
      elif cycle%5==2: all forward
      elif cycle%5==3: all backward
      else: random_batch
      improved=False; changed=False
      if random:
         for _ in range(random_batch): if time>... break; p,q random; pair_coordinate(...)
      else:
         for p in range(...): pair_coordinate
      if improved: no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve+=1
         if no_improve>=12: break
      cycle+=1
   return h, compute_c5(h,n)
```
For n=700, one all pass 699 pairs ~ maybe 1s; 5 cycles ~5s; time 400s -> 80 cycles. Good.
Random_batch for n=700 maybe 200 trials; if improved break? Need count. Use random_batch scaled: int(min(200, max(20, 20000//n)))? For n=700, 28; n=200, 200. But random pairs can be many. To escape, use 100 for all. We'll set batch = max(50, 20000//n) maybe 200 for 100, 50 for 400. But random pair one coordinate subproblem same cost as one pair; 50*700? okay. Generating starts algorithmically:
```
def make_binary_start(n, seed):
   h=np.zeros(n); idx=np.random.RandomState(seed).choice(n, size=n//2, replace=False); h[idx]=1; return h
def make_pattern_start(n, seed):
   rng=...
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   kind=rng.randint(5)
   if kind==0: h=np.where(np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.randint(1,6)*x/2 + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))>0,1,0)
   else fractional sine: h=0.5+0.5*np.sin(freq*x+phase); h=clip
   elif sawtooth: h=(np.arange(n)%period) < period//2? etc
   project
```

Interpolation from best/parent:
```
def interpolate_h(h_old,n_new):
  if len==n_new return copy
  x_old=np.linspace(0,2,len(h_old)); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new)
  h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old)
  return project_feasible(h,n_new)
```

Detailed functions:

1. `compute_c5(h,n)`: dx=2.0/n; return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)

2. `project_feasible(h,n)`: target=n/2.0. Use parent algorithm but faster? It can solve sum with clipping by adjusting. For large iterations maybe. We'll use simpler: clip, diff=target-sum; if diff>0, add to entries with room weighted; but this may not converge if room small. Use loop 1000.
```
for _ in range(1000):
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 if diff>0:
   room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
   if tot<1e-15: break
   h=np.clip(h+diff*room/tot,0,1)
 else:
   tot=h.sum()
   if tot<1e-15: break
   h=np.clip(h+diff*h/tot,0,1)
```
This is not exact Euclidean but okay. For fractional sums, can converge.

```
def pair_find_a(Q0,Q1,Q2,diag_idx,Alo,Ahi,old_a,low,high,iters=30,mode=0):
    if Ahi - Alo < 1e-12: return Alo
    tol=1e-9
    M=len(Q0)
    non=np.ones(M,bool)
    if diag_idx>=0 and diag_idx<M: non[diag_idx]=False
    q0=Q0[non]; q1=Q1[non]; q2=Q2[non]
    lin=np.abs(q2)<tol
    pos=lin & (q1>tol)
    neg=lin & (q1<-tol)
    zero=lin & ~(pos|neg)  # careful ~ works bool; zero=lin & (np.abs(q1)<=tol)
    zero_max = float(np.max(q0[zero])) if np.any(zero) else 0.0
    conv = q2 > tol
    c0=q0[conv]; c1=q1[conv]; c2=q2[conv]
    conc = q2 < -tol
    conc_idx = np.flatnonzero(conc)
    d_exists = diag_idx>=0 and diag_idx<M
    if d_exists: d0=Q0[diag_idx]; d1=Q1[diag_idx]; d2=Q2[diag_idx]
    low = max(low, zero_max)
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=(low+high)*0.5
       ok=True
       if mid < zero_max - 1e-10: ok=False
       if ok:
          lo=Alo; hi=Ahi
          if conv.any():
             disc=c1*c1 - 4*c2*(c0-mid)
             if np.any(disc < -1e-8): ok=False
             else:
                disc=np.maximum(disc,0.0)
                sq=np.sqrt(disc)
                r1=(-c1-sq)/(2*c2); r2=(-c1+sq)/(2*c2)
                if r1.size:
                   lo=max(lo,float(np.max(r1)))
                   hi=min(hi,float(np.min(r2)))
                   if lo>hi+1e-10: ok=False
          if ok:
             if pos.any(): hi=min(hi,float(np.min((mid-q0[pos])/q1[pos])))
             if neg.any(): lo=max(lo,float(np.max((mid-q0[neg])/q1[neg])))
             if lo>hi+1e-10: ok=False
          if ok and conc.any():
             intervals=[(lo,hi)]
             for idx in conc_idx:
                a0=q0[idx]; a1=q1[idx]; a2=q2[idx]
                disc=a1*a1 - 4*a2*(a0-mid)
                if disc >= -1e-8:
                   sq=np.sqrt(max(disc,0.0)); r1=(-a1-sq)/(2*a2); r2=(-a1+sq)/(2*a2); if r1>r2 swap
                   new_int=[]
                   for l,hh in intervals:
                      if l <= min(hh,r1)+1e-10: new_int.append((l,min(hh,r1)))
                      l2=max(l,r2)
                      if l2 <= hh+1e-10: new_int.append((l2,hh))
                   intervals=new_int
                   if not intervals: break
                # else concave no restriction
             if not intervals: ok=False
             # store intervals? For diag later, not in this simple variable. We'll just if diag use first? If conc exists, need apply diag to all intervals. Could code but conc rare.
          if ok and d_exists and d2 < -tol:
             disc=d1*d1 - 4*d2*(d0-mid)
             if disc >= -1e-8:
                sq=np.sqrt(max(disc,0.0)); r1=...; r2=...
                # if conc intervals? If conc.any(), need check any interval outside. For simplicity, if conc.any(), we can after applying diag to first intervals? Let's handle by reducing to a list.
```

Pseudo pair_optimize:
```
def pair_optimize(h, n, time_budget, seed, batch=100, lateral_every=5):
    h = h.copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    t_end=time.time()+time_budget
    no=0; cyc=0; lateral_cycle=0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    while time.time() < t_end:
       improved=0; lateral=0
       phase = cyc % 6
       if phase==0: pairs = range(0,n-1,2)
       elif phase==1: pairs=range(1,n-1,2)
       elif phase==2: pairs=range(0,n-1)
       elif phase==3: pairs=range(n-2,-1,-1)
       else:
          # random batch
          for _ in range(batch):
             if time.time()>t_end: break
             # choose p,q
             p=rng.randint(n)
             q=rng.randint(n)
             s=h[p]+h[q]
             if s <=1e-10 or s >=2-1e-10 or p==q: continue
             # to move mass, if h[p]<0.5 try q with >; not necessary
             res=pair_coordinate(h,n,p,q,c, mode=1 if cyc%3==0 else 0)
             if res==1: improved+=1
             elif res==2: lateral+=1
       if not random_phase:
          for p in pairs: ...
       if improved==0 and lateral==0: no+=1
       else: no=0
       if no>=12: break
       cyc+=1
    c5=compute_c5(h,n)
    # ensure sum exact
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    return h,c5
```
Need define pairs not using range in conditional with negative? okay.

else random
```

Let's design simple SLSQP top-level without closures:
```
def smooth_obj(z, n, alpha):
   c = np.correlate(z,1-z,'full'); m=c.max(); s=alpha*(c-m); mx=s.max(); return (m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx)))/alpha)*(2.0/n)
def sum_g(z,n): return z.sum()-n/2.0
def sum_jac(z,n): return np.ones(n)
def slsqp_polish(h,n, alphas, maxiter, deadline):
   best=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_g,'jac':sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      try:
         res=minimize(smooth_obj, best, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-18})
         new=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=compute_c5(new,n)
         if c<best_c -1e-12: best=new; best_c=c
      except Exception: pass
   return best,best_c
```
No closures. But `bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n` for n=300 okay. SLSQP numerical gradient expensive. We can limit n<=150. Not sure if worth. Could include if time remains after pair_cd on best. But extra code ~20 lines.

However SLSQP objective has non-smooth max? Smoothed. It might increase C by moving to smooth minimum; we check. Might break sum? constraint. Could find better.

But `minimize` may not be available if scipy not? It is. Need import. If SLSQP takes long, respect deadline: set maxiter small and check after; inside minimize can't interrupt. For n=150, maxiter 100 maybe okay. But if it hangs, could eat budget. We can use try and time? Can't interrupt. Set very conservative: for n<=120, maxiter=80. For n<=200, maybe 50. Only if >120s left.

```
def make_pattern_start(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    kind=int(rng.randint(4))
    if kind==0:
       f=float(rng.randint(1,6))
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h=(np.sin(f*x+ph)>0).astype(float)
    elif kind==1:
       f=float(rng.randint(1,5))
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h=0.5+0.5*np.sin(f*x+ph)
    elif kind==2:
       # blocks of lengths via fractional Beatty
       a=rng.uniform(0.3,0.7)
       h=((np.arange(n)*a)%1.0)<0.5
    else:
       h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,size=n//2,replace=False); h[idx]=1
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
For n odd, size n//2 then project to n/2 (float .5) by adjusting fractional. Good.

Let's write pair_find_a robust:
```
def pair_find_a(Q0,Q1,Q2,diag_idx,Alo,Ahi,old_a,low,high,iters=32,mode=0):
    if Ahi - Alo <= 1e-12: return Alo
    tol=1e-9
    M=Q0.size
    non = np.ones(M, dtype=bool)
    if 0 <= diag_idx < M: non[diag_idx]=False
    if non.sum()==0:
       return old_a
    q0=Q0[non]; q1=Q1[non]; q2=Q2[non]
    if np.any(q2 < -tol):
       return old_a
    lin=np.abs(q2)<tol
    pos=lin & (q1 > tol)
    neg=lin & (q1 < -tol)
    zero=lin & ~(pos|neg)
    zero_max = float(np.max(q0[zero])) if np.any(zero) else 0.0
    conv = q2 > tol
    c0=q0[conv] if np.any(conv) else np.empty(0)
    ...
    pos0 = q0[pos] if np.any(pos) else np.empty(0)
    ...
    low = max(low, zero_max, 0.0)
    # Ensure high > low? if high<=low: high=low+1e-10
    if high <= low: high = low + 1e-10
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=...
       ok=True
       if mid < zero_max - 1e-10: ok=False
       lo=Alo; hi=Ahi
       if ok and conv_any:
          disc = c1*c1 - 4.0*c2*(c0 - mid)
          if np.any(disc < -1e-8): ok=False
          else:
             disc=np.maximum(disc,0.0)
             sq=np.sqrt(disc)
             r1=(-c1-sq)/(2.0*c2)
             r2=(-c1+sq)/(2.0*c2)
             if c0.size:
                lo=max(lo, float(np.max(r1)))
                hi=min(hi, float(np.min(r2)))
                if lo > hi + 1e-10: ok=False
       if ok and pos_any:
          hi = min(hi, float(np.min((mid - pos0)/pos1)))
       if ok and neg_any:
          lo = max(lo, float(np.max((mid - neg0)/neg1)))
       if ok and lo > hi + 1e-10: ok=False
       if ok and d_exists:
          if d2 < -tol:
             disc = d1*d1 - 4.0*d2*(d0 - mid)
             if disc >= -1e-8:
                sq=np.sqrt(max(disc,0.0))
                r1=(-d1-sq)/(2.0*d2); r2=(-d1+sq)/(2.0*d2)
                if r1 > r2: r1,r2=r2,r1
                if not (lo <= min(hi, r1) + 1e-10 or max(lo, r2) <= hi + 1e-10):
                   ok=False
          elif d1 > tol: hi=min(hi,(mid-d0)/d1); ...
       if ok: high=mid
       else: low=mid
    L=high
    # final intervals: recompute base lo/hi with L to avoid using last mid maybe.
    lo=Alo; hi=Ahi; ok=True
    if L < zero_max - 1e-10: ok=False
    if ok and conv_any: ...
    if ok and pos_any... etc
    if not ok: return old_a
    intervals=[]
    if d_exists and d2 < -tol:
       disc=...
       if disc >= -1e-8:
          roots; split [lo,hi]
       else: intervals.append((lo,hi))
    else:
       intervals.append((lo,hi))
    # filter valid
    best_pt=None; best_score=inf
    for l,hh in intervals:
       if hh - l < -1e-12: continue
       if mode == 0:
          if old_a < l: pt=l; dist=l-old_a
          elif old_a > hh: pt=hh; dist=old_a-hh
          else: pt=old_a; dist=0.0
          score=dist
       else:
          mid_interval=0.5*(l+hh)
          # choose interval with largest length? to move; score = -length + small distance
          score=-(hh-l)
       if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_pt=pt
    if best_pt is None: return old_a
    return float(np.clip(best_pt, Alo, Ahi))
```

...

```
This prevents endless lateral.

project_feasible:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final exact? If slightly off, adjust one variable
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
       room=1-h; idx=np.argmax(room); if room[idx]>1e-12: h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff) ... but may sum.
```
The iterative projection might not hit exact within 1e-14. The harness likely checks close? It says sum == n_points/2 exactly? Floating exact not possible; they probably tolerance. But to be safe, after loop, adjust a coordinate with room to satisfy target.
```
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 1e-14:
       room = 1.0 - h
       order = np.argsort(-room)
       add = min(diff, room[order[0]])
       h[order[0]] += add
       diff -= add
       if diff > 1e-14: h[order[1]] = min(1, h[order[1]]+diff)
    elif diff < -1e-14: similar subtract from largest h.
```
But if one step not enough, loop maybe. Since target and sum close, one variable enough if room enough. For diff could be >1? No after projection close. We'll do simple:
```
    for idx in range(n): if diff>0 and room[idx]>=diff: h[idx]+=diff; break ...
```
But loop O(n) fine.
Need maintain [0,1]. We'll implement robust:
```
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       if diff > 0:
          order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
          for idx in order:
             room = 1.0 - h[idx]
             if room > 1e-15:
                step = min(room, diff)
                h[idx] += step; diff -= step
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
       else:
          order = np.argsort(h)  # smallest? To subtract, use smallest h to avoid below0? Actually need reduce sum, subtract from variables with high value? If we subtract from smallest, may hit0 quickly. Use high values. order = np.argsort(-h)
          for idx in order:
             val = h[idx]
             if val > 1e-15:
                step = min(val, -diff)
                h[idx] -= step; diff += step
                if abs(diff)<=1e-14: break
```

```
def pair_contrib(h, p, q, k, vp, vq, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq,
                 vp_mask, vq_mask, vip_mask, viq_mask,
                 sp_jp, sq_jp, sp_jq, sq_jq):
    val_jp = base_jp + vp*sp_jp + vq*sq_jp
    val_jq = base_jq + vp*sp_jq + vq*sq_jq
    return (vp_mask * vp * (1.0 - val_jp) + vq_mask * vq * (1.0 - val_jq) +
            vip_mask * base_ip * (1.0 - vp) + viq_mask * base_iq * (1.0 - vq))
```
Note `h` arg unused; remove to shorten. It only uses arrays. `def pair_contrib(vp, vq, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq, ...):`

```
def pair_optimize(h, n, time_budget, seed, batch=100):
    if n < 2 or time_budget <= 0:
       return h, compute_c5(h,n)
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t_end = time.time() + time_budget
    stall = 0
    cyc = 0
    while time.time() < t_end:
        improved = 0
        lateral = 0
        phase = cyc % 6
        if phase == 0:
            mode=0; rng_pairs = range(0, n-1, 2)
        elif phase == 1:
            mode=0; rng_pairs = range(1, n-1, 2)
        elif phase == 2:
            mode=0; rng_pairs = range(0, n-1)
        elif phase == 3:
            mode=0; rng_pairs = range(n-2, -1, -1)
        else:
            mode = 1 if phase == 4 else 0? random each.
```

```
def make_binary_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
       m = int(np.floor(n/2.0))
       idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
For odd n, project will set one partial.

`make_pattern_start`:
```
def make_pattern_start(n, seed):
    rng=...
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    kind=int(rng.randint(5))
    if kind == 0:
       f=float(rng.randint(1,7)); ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h=(np.sin(f*x+ph)>0).astype(float)
    elif kind == 1:
       f=float(rng.randint(1,5)); ph=...
       h=0.5+0.5*np.sin(f*x+ph)
    elif kind == 2:
       f=float(rng.randint(1,4)); ph=...
       h=0.5+0.4*np.sin(f*x+ph)+0.2*np.cos(2*f*x)
    elif kind == 3:
       a=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); h=((np.arange(n)*a)%1.0)<0.5
    else:
       period=int(rng.randint(3,9)); h=(np.arange(n) % period) < (period//2)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
period//2 if period odd? For density half approximate. Good.
Kind 2 may exceed [0,1], clip in project. Good.

`interpolate_h`:
```
def interpolate_h(h_old, n_new):
    old_n=len(h_old)
    if old_n == n_new:
       return h_old.copy()
    if old_n < 2:
       return make_binary_start(n_new, 1)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,old_n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old), n_new)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(250):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if diff > 1e-14:
        order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        for idx in order:
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            if room > 1e-15:
                step = min(room, diff)
                h[idx] += step
                diff -= step
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                    break
    elif diff < -1e-14:
        order = np.argsort(-h)
        for idx in order:
            val = h[idx]
            if val > 1e-15:
                step = min(val, -diff)
                h[idx] -= step
                diff += step
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                    break
    return h


def pair_contrib(vp, vq, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq,
                 vp_mask, vq_mask, vip_mask, viq_mask,
                 sp_jp, sq_jp, sp_jq, sq_jq):
    val_jp = base_jp + vp * sp_jp + vq * sq_jp
    val_jq = base_jq + vp * sp_jq + vq * sq_jq
    return (vp_mask * vp * (1.0 - val_jp)
            + vq_mask * vq * (1.0 - val_jq)
            + vip_mask * base_ip * (1.0 - vp)
            + viq_mask * base_iq * (1.0 - vq))


def pair_find_a(Q0, Q1, Q2, diag_idx, Alo, Ahi, old_a, low, high, iters=32, mode=0):
    if Ahi - Alo <= 1e-12:
        return float(np.clip(Alo, Alo, Ahi))
    tol = 1e-9
    M = Q0.size
    non = np.ones(M, dtype=bool)
    if 0 <= diag_idx < M:
        non[diag_idx] = False
    if not non.any():
        return float(np.clip(old_a, Alo, Ahi))

    q0 = Q0[non]
    q1 = Q1[non]
    q2 = Q2[non]
    if np.any(q2 < -tol):
        return float(np.clip(old_a, Alo, Ahi))

    lin = np.abs(q2) < tol
    pos = lin & (q1 > tol)
    neg = lin & (q1 < -tol)
    zero = lin & (np.abs(q1) <= tol)

    zero_any = bool(zero.any())
    zero_max = float(np.max(q0[zero])) if zero_any else 0.0

    conv = q2 > tol
    conv_any = bool(conv.any())
    if conv_any:
        c0 = q0[conv]
        c1 = q1[conv]
        c2 = q2[conv]

    pos_any = bool(pos.any())
    if pos_any:
        pos0 = q0[pos]
        pos1 = q1[pos]
    neg_any = bool(neg.any())
    if neg_any:
        neg0 = q0[neg]
        neg1 = q1[neg]

    d_exists = 0 <= diag_idx < M
    if d_exists:
        d0 = Q0[diag_idx]
        d1 = Q1[diag_idx]
        d2 = Q2[diag_idx]

    low = max(low, zero_max, 0.0)
    if high <= low:
        high = low + 1e-10

    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        ok = True
        if mid < zero_max - 1e-10:
            ok = False
        if ok:
            lo = Alo
            hi = Ahi
            if conv_any:
                disc = c1 * c1 - 4.0 * c2 * (c0 - mid)
                if np.any(disc < -1e-8):
                    ok = False
                else:
                    disc = np.maximum(disc, 0.0)
                    sq = np.sqrt(disc)
                    r1 = (-c1 - sq) / (2.0 * c2)
                    r2 = (-c1 + sq) / (2.0 * c2)
                    lo = max(lo, float(np.max(r1)))
                    hi = min(hi, float(np.min(r2)))
                    if lo > hi + 1e-10:
                        ok = False
            if ok and pos_any:
                hi = min(hi, float(np.min((mid - pos0) / pos1)))
            if ok and neg_any:
                lo = max(lo, float(np.max((mid - neg0) / neg1)))
            if ok and lo > hi + 1e-10:
                ok = False
            if ok and d_exists:
                if d2 < -tol:
                    disc = d1 * d1 - 4.0 * d2 * (d0 - mid)
                    if disc >= -1e-8:
                        sq = np.sqrt(max(disc, 0.0))
                        r1 = (-d1 - sq) / (2.0 * d2)
                        r2 = (-d1 + sq) / (2.0 * d2)
                        if r1 > r2:
                            r1, r2 = r2, r1
                        if not (lo <= min(hi, r1) + 1e-10 or max(lo, r2) <= hi + 1e-10):
                            ok = False
                elif d1 > tol:
                    hi = min(hi, (mid - d0) / d1)
                    if hi < lo - 1e-12:
                        ok = False
                elif d1 < -tol:
                    lo = max(lo, (mid - d0) / d1)
                    if lo > hi + 1e-12:
                        ok = False
                elif d0 > mid + 1e-10:
                    ok = False
        if ok:
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid

    L = high
    ok = True
    lo = Alo
    hi = Ahi
    if L < zero_max - 1e-10:
        ok = False
    if ok and conv_any:
        disc = c1 * c1 - 4.0 * c2 * (c0 - L)
        if np.any(disc < -1e-8):
            ok = False
        else:
            disc = np.maximum(disc, 0.0)
            sq = np.sqrt(disc)
            r1 = (-c1 - sq) / (2.0 * c2)
            r2 = (-c1 + sq) / (2.0 * c2)
            lo = max(lo, float(np.max(r1)))
            hi = min(hi, float(np.min(r2)))
            if lo > hi + 1e-10:
                ok = False
    if ok and pos_any:
        hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - pos0) / pos1)))
    if ok and neg_any:
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - neg0) / neg1)))
    if ok and lo > hi + 1e-10:
        ok = False
    if ok and d_exists and d2 >= -tol:
        if d1 > tol:
            hi = min(hi, (L - d0) / d1)
        elif d1 < -tol:
            lo = max(lo, (L - d0) / d1)
        elif d0 > L + 1e-10:
            ok = False
        if lo > hi + 1e-10:
            ok = False
    if not ok:
        return float(np.clip(old_a, Alo, Ahi))

    intervals = []
    if d_exists and d2 < -tol:
        disc = d1 * d1 - 4.0 * d2 * (d0 - L)
        if disc >= -1e-8:
            sq = np.sqrt(max(disc, 0.0))
            r1 = (-d1 - sq) / (2.0 * d2)
            r2 = (-d1 + sq) / (2.0 * d2)
            if r1 > r2:
                r1, r2 = r2, r1
            h2 = min(hi, r1)
            if lo <= h2 + 1e-12:
                intervals.append((lo, h2))
            l2 = max(lo, r2)
            if l2 <= hi + 1e-12:
                intervals.append((l2, hi))
        else:
            intervals.append((lo, hi))
    else:
        intervals.append((lo, hi))

    best_pt = None
    best_score = 1e100
    for l, hh in intervals:
        l = max(l, Alo)
        hh = min(hh, Ahi)
        if hh < l - 1e-12:
            continue
        if mode == 0:
            if old_a < l:
                pt = l
                score = l - old_a
            elif old_a > hh:
                pt = hh
                score = old_a - hh
            else:
                pt = old_a
                score = 0.0
        else:
            length = max(0.0, hh - l)
            pt = 0.5 * (l + hh)
            score = -length + 1e-6 * abs(pt - old_a)
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_pt = pt

    if best_pt is None:
        return float(np.clip(old_a, Alo, Ahi))
    return float(np.clip(best_pt, Alo, Ahi))


def pair_coordinate(h, n, p, q, c, mode=0):
    if p == q:
        return 0
    p = int(p)
    q = int(q)
    if p < 0 or p >= n or q < 0 or q >= n:
        return 0
    s = float(h[p] + h[q])
    Alo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    Ahi = min(1.0, s)
    if Ahi - Alo < 1e-12:
        return 0

    old_vp = float(np.clip(h[p], 0.0, 1.0))
    old_vq = float(np.clip(h[q], 0.0, 1.0))

    m_lo = min(n - 1 - p, n - 1 - q)
    m_hi = max(2 * n - 2 - p, 2 * n - 2 - q)
    ms = np.arange(m_lo, m_hi + 1, dtype=int)
    k = ms - (n - 1)
    M = ms.size

    jp = p + k
    vjp = (jp >= 0) & (jp < n)
    sp_jp = ((jp == p) & vjp)
    sq_jp = ((jp == q) & vjp)
    base_jp = np.zeros(M)
    if vjp.any():
        idx = jp[vjp]
        base_jp[vjp] = h[idx]
        base_jp[sp_jp | sq_jp] = 0.0
    sp_jp = sp_jp.astype(float)
    sq_jp = sq_jp.astype(float)

    jq = q + k
    vjq = (jq >= 0) & (jq < n)
    sp_jq = ((jq == p) & vjq)
    sq_jq = ((jq == q) & vjq)
    base_jq = np.zeros(M)
    if vjq.any():
        idx = jq[vjq]
        base_jq[vjq] = h[idx]
        base_jq[sp_jq | sq_jq] = 0.0
    sp_jq = sp_jq.astype(float)
    sq_jq = sq_jq.astype(float)

    ip = p - k
    vip_all = (ip >= 0) & (ip < n)
    vip = vip_all & (ip != p) & (ip != q)
    base_ip = np.zeros(M)
    if vip.any():
        base_ip[vip] = h[ip[vip]]
    vip_mask = vip.astype(float)

    iq = q - k
    viq_all = (iq >= 0) & (iq < n)
    viq = viq_all & (iq != p) & (iq != q)
    base_iq = np.zeros(M)
    if viq.any():
        base_iq[viq] = h[iq[viq]]
    viq_mask = viq.astype(float)

    vp_mask = vjp.astype(float)
    vq_mask = vjq.astype(float)

    old_contrib = pair_contrib(old_vp, old_vq, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq,
                               vp_mask, vq_mask, vip_mask, viq_mask,
                               sp_jp, sq_jp, sp_jq, sq_jq)
    f_base = c[ms] - old_contrib

    f0 = f_base + pair_contrib(0.0, s, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq,
                               vp_mask, vq_mask, vip_mask, viq_mask,
                               sp_jp, sq_jp, sp_jq, sq_jq)
    f1 = f_base + pair_contrib(0.5, s - 0.5, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq,
                               vp_mask, vq_mask, vip_mask, viq_mask,
                               sp_jp, sq_jp, sp_jq, sq_jq)
    f2 = f_base + pair_contrib(1.0, s - 1.0, base_jp, base_jq, base_ip, base_iq,
                               vp_mask, vq_mask, vip_mask, viq_mask,
                               sp_jp, sq_jp, sp_jq, sq_jq)

    Q0 = f0
    Q1 = 4.0 * f1 - 3.0 * f0 - f2
    Q2 = 2.0 * f0 - 4.0 * f1 + 2.0 * f2

    dpos = np.flatnonzero(ms == n - 1)
    diag_idx = int(dpos[0]) if dpos.size else -1

    inside_max = float(np.max(c[ms]))
    outside_max = 0.0
    Lfull = 2 * n - 2
    if m_lo > 0:
        outside_max = max(outside_max, float(np.max(c[:m_lo])))
    if m_hi < Lfull:
        outside_max = max(outside_max, float(np.max(c[m_hi + 1:])))
    current_max = max(inside_max, outside_max)

    if mode == 0 and outside_max >= current_max - 1e-10:
        return 0

    val_old = float(np.max(Q0 + Q1 * old_vp + Q2 * old_vp * old_vp))
    high = max(current_max, val_old, outside_max) + 1e-10
    low = max(outside_max, 0.0)

    a_new = pair_find_a(Q0, Q1, Q2, diag_idx, Alo, Ahi, old_vp, low, high, 32, mode)
    if abs(a_new - old_vp) < 1e-12:
        return 0

    a_new = float(np.clip(a_new, Alo, Ahi))
    b_new = s - a_new
    if b_new < 0.0:
        b_new = 0.0
        a_new = s
    elif b_new > 1.0:
        b_new = 1.0
        a_new = s - 1.0
    a_new = float(np.clip(a_new, Alo, Ahi))
    b_new = s - a_new

    new_vals = Q0 + Q1 * a_new + Q2 * a_new * a_new
    if np.any(new_vals < -1e-7):
        return 0
    new_vals = np.where(new_vals < 0.0, 0.0, new_vals)
    new_local = max(float(np.max(new_vals)), outside_max)
    if new_local > current_max + 1e-8:
        return 0

    h[p] = a_new
    h[q] = b_new
    c[ms] = new_vals

    if new_local < current_max - 1e-10:
        return 1
    return 2


def pair_optimize(h, n, time_budget, seed, batch=100):
    if n < 2:
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t_end = time.time() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    stall = 0
    cyc = 0

    while time.time() < t_end:
        improved = 0
        phase = cyc % 6
        if phase == 0:
            seq = range(0, n - 1, 2)
            mode = 0
        elif phase == 1:
            seq = range(1, n - 1, 2)
            mode = 0
        elif phase == 2:
            seq = range(0, n - 1)
            mode = 0
        elif phase == 3:
            seq = range(n - 2, -1, -1)
            mode = 0
        elif phase == 4:
            seq = None
            mode = 1
        else:
            seq = range(0, n - 1)
            mode = 1

        if seq is not None:
            for p in seq:
                if time.time() >= t_end:
                    break
                res = pair_coordinate(h, n, p, p + 1, c, mode)
                if res == 1:
                    improved += 1
        else:
            one = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
            zero = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5)
            for _ in range(batch):
                if time.time() >= t_end:
                    break
                if one.size and zero.size and rng.rand() < 0.8:
                    p = int(one[rng.randint(one.size)])
                    q = int(zero[rng.randint(zero.size)])
                else:
                    p = int(rng.randint(n))
                    q = int(rng.randint(n))
                if p == q:
                    continue
                s = h[p] + h[q]
                if s <= 1e-10 or s >= 2.0 - 1e-10:
                    continue
                res = pair_coordinate(h, n, p, q, c, 1)
                if res == 1:
                    improved += 1

        if improved > 0:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
        if stall > 25:
            break

        cyc += 1
        if cyc % 32 == 0:
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    return h, compute_c5(h, n)


def interpolate_h(h_old, n_new):
    old_n = len(h_old)
    if old_n == n_new:
        return h_old.copy()
    if old_n < 2:
        n_new = int(n_new)
        h = np.zeros(n_new)
        m = int(np.floor(n_new / 2.0))
        idx = np.random.RandomState(1).choice(n_new, size=m, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old), n_new)


def make_binary_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = int(np.floor(n / 2.0))
    if m > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_pattern_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    kind = int(rng.randint(6))
    if kind == 0:
        f = float(rng.randint(1, 7))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h = (np.sin(f * x + ph) > 0.0).astype(float)
    elif kind == 1:
        f = float(rng.randint(1, 5))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(f * x + ph)
    elif kind == 2:
        f = float(rng.randint(1, 4))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(f * x + ph) + 0.2 * np.cos(2.0 * f * x)
    elif kind == 3:
        a = rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8)
        h = ((np.arange(n) * a) % 1.0) < 0.5
    elif kind == 4:
        period = int(rng.randint(3, 9))
        h = (np.arange(n) % period) < (period // 2)
    else:
        h = make_binary_start(n, seed + 1)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 10.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1.0
    best_n = 0
    parent_h = None
    parent_n = 0

    try:
        h_parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        parent_n = len(h_parent)
        if parent_n >= 2:
            parent_h = project_feasible(h_parent, parent_n)
            c0 = compute_c5(parent_h, parent_n)
            best_h = parent_h.copy()
            best_c = c0
            best_n = parent_n
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    if parent_h is not None:
        parent_budget = min(0.42 * float(budget_s), 420.0)
        if time.time() + 5.0 < deadline:
            h_opt, c_opt = pair_optimize(
                parent_h, parent_n, parent_budget, seed + 1,
                batch=max(30, 30000 // max(2, parent_n))
            )
            if c_opt < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h_opt, c_opt, parent_n

    n_list = [500, 350, 250, 200, 150, 100]
    for n in n_list:
        if time.time() > deadline - (120.0 if n > 300 else 45.0):
            break
        starts = []
        if best_h is not None and best_n != n:
            h_int = interpolate_h(best_h, n)
            starts.append(h_int)
            if n <= 300:
                starts.append(project_feasible(1.0 - h_int, n))
        if parent_h is not None and parent_n != n:
            starts.append(interpolate_h(parent_h, n))
        starts.append(make_binary_start(n, seed + 1000 + n))
        starts.append(make_pattern_start(n, seed + 2000 + n))
        if n <= 200:
            starts.append(make_binary_start(n, seed + 3000 + n))

        if n > 450:
            tb = 70.0
        elif n > 300:
            tb = 55.0
        elif n > 180:
            tb = 45.0
        else:
            tb = 32.0

        for si in range(len(starts)):
            if time.time() > deadline - 25.0:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.time() - 20.0
            if rem <= 0.0:
                break
            tb_use = min(tb, rem)
            h_s = project_feasible(starts[si], n)
            h_o, c_o = pair_optimize(
                h_s, n, tb_use, seed + 5000 + 7 * n + 13 * si,
                batch=max(30, 30000 // max(2, n))
            )
            if c_o < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h_o, c_o, n

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        tb = min(120.0, 0.45 * rem)
        if tb > 30.0:
            h_noise = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, best_n)
            h_noise = project_feasible(h_noise, best_n)
            h_o, c_o = pair_optimize(
                h_noise, best_n, tb, seed + 9000,
                batch=max(30, 30000 // max(2, best_n))
            )
            if c_o < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h_o, c_o, best_n

    if best_h is None:
        n = 200
        h = make_binary_start(n, seed)
        h, best_c = pair_optimize(h, n, max(20.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0), seed, batch=200)
        best_h = h
        best_n = n

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
